# Trading Research

- Andrea does trading research as a serious hobby alongside medical school.
- Style: manual technical analysis on weekly charts, notably Elliott Wave counts (e.g. NASDAQ 100 E-mini futures, TradingView, CME).
- Time budget agreed for trading research + websites combined: 10-20 hours/week, never at the expense of medical studies.
- Deep-work preference for this work: evenings, plus part of weekends.
- Built a Pine v5 indicator "AVFI v3 vs v2 [spread axis]": compares AVFI v3 (range-aware vol floor via Parkinson-style sigmaPk, log-volume normalization va) against a slow EMA of AVFI v2; visualizes the spread as a ribbon and as a sigma-normalized histogram (z-score over a rolling window). Reference copy stored durably in pipeline/strategies/avfi_v3_reference.pine (working-tree path /home/user/projects/quant/strategies/avfi_v3_reference.pine is ephemeral).
- [AVFI-NQ Weekly Strategy and Research Programme v1.0](avfi-nq-strategy-programme.md) — the governing document for the strategy phase, written Nov 2026 after the 20-book study programme closed. Synthesises the book layers (Elliott Wave structure, VSA/microstructure flow, econometrics, execution, sizing, validation epistemics) around the AVFI v3 spread indicator; contains the pre-registered hypothesis registry (H1-H6), candidate rule sets A/B/C, the 9-stage validation pipeline (data, pandas replication, event study, base-case, sensitivity, costs, multiple-testing-adjusted reliability, 26-week paper-trading forward test, go/no-go), risk and sizing rules, the weekly operating cadence, and honest limitations.
- Pipeline status (Nov 2026): stages 0-6a complete (full verdict log in pipeline/research/PROGRESS.md). Rule set A killed: A0 state system Sharpe -0.115 in-sample (worse than random-entry control) and 0.420 validation (equal to random, 1/8 of buy-hold upside at 37% exposure); the pre-registered |sp_z|>=1 variant is structurally vacuous (transitions occur at spread≈0 so sp_z≈0: zero trades ever). H1/H2 not supported; H4 v3 does not dominate v2; H5 no parameter plateau (11% of grid, pure slow-EMA drift artifact); H6 all conditional structure is regime-concentrated (bear-capitulation bounce in momentum_down, +0.23 sigma next-week, +2.84% 4-week, p=0.03, but one 2008-2010 episode). Stage 5: costs immaterial at weekly horizon (3-tick slippage moves Sharpe <= 0.01). Stage 6a: searched 27-combo grid family fails the White reality check (family best 0.58, RC p=0.11); every AVFI variant fails individually; the only variant significant in both windows is the 12-week momentum control (Sharpe 0.798 IS p=0.033; 0.508 VAL p=0.053), consistent with the generic trend-following prior. Reading: AVFI is a slow regime descriptor, not a weekly timer. Holdout 2021+ still sealed, never touched. Stage 4b (T robustness, unsealed data): full-span Sharpe 0.571, survives every leave-one-year-out (0.445-0.686) and full 2008-09 exclusion (0.50), max DD -23.5% vs buy-hold -52%, beats buy-hold in only 3/14 years (defensive profile, earns in bad years). Stage 6b: the single-open holdout battery is implemented exactly per Amendment 1 and hard-gated behind an explicit go argument; it has never been run. The programme waits on Andrea's go or veto on opening the battery; secondary waits: H3 wave annotations (spec in pipeline/docs/h3_annotation_spec.md), TradingView replication cross-check, broker/CME export for 1999-2000. Paper-trading Sunday log template ready (pipeline/docs/stage7_sunday_log_template.md). Stage 6c (pre-battery power analysis, unsealed data only, 11 Nov 2026): holdout window 307 weeks; the battery bootstrap passes at observed Sharpe ~0.75; power at T's unsealed Sharpe 0.571 is 43% (80% power needs true Sharpe ~1.0-1.1); false-pass rate ~5%; waiting to end-2028 lifts power only to ~58%. Occupancy-only readout (no returns, no rules evaluated): momentum_down weeks 2021+ = 0, so the H7 gate will fail and the battery reduces to the single T test with no multiplicity penalty. Decision brief with go/wait/veto framing at pipeline/docs/battery_go_veto_brief.md. Stage 0d (source cross-check, unsealed window only, 11 Nov 2026): canonical NQ weekly bars vs Yahoo ^NDX index, 1057 overlap weeks, correlation 0.982, sign agreement 95.8%, median weekly |diff| 0.14%; all 11 divergence weeks beyond p99 explained by quarterly roll/expiry windows or crisis-basis episodes (Dec 2000, 9/11, GFC, Dec 2018, COVID); no corrupt prints; canonical series fit for the battery; caveat same vendor both series; 1999-2000 gap still needs Andrea's broker/CME export (usable start would move ~20 months earlier, only under a new amendment). research/verify_all.sh added: one-command post-reset verification incl. stage6b seal check. Stage 4c (strategy upgrade, 11 Nov 2026, Andrea instruction 'T is too basic', unsealed data only): kept T's frozen 12-week signal, varied only position sizing with pre-committed constants (vol window 8, target 20% annualized, reversal_down throttle 0.5, cap 1.0, no scans; close-to-close convention, costs on |dw|). T binary baseline 0.626 Sharpe / -21.5% maxDD / +217% / LOYOmin 0.505; U1 vol-target 0.639 / -21.5%; U2 AVFI throttle 0.646 / -15.5% (misses LOYO rule by 0.001); U3 combined 0.639 / -14.2% / +148% / 0.516, Bonferroni x3 p 0.015, the only candidate passing the pre-committed rule. Honest reading: Sharpe edge small, drawdown edge crisis-driven (excl 2008-09: U3 0.560 vs T 0.565), defensive insurance paid with upside; in-sample selection risk real; AVFI finally has a measurable job as a risk throttle, not an entry timer. Proposed Amendment 2 (docs/amendment2_U3_sizing.md, pending Andrea's sign-off) adds U3 to the single-open battery (family T+U3, max-t); research/stage6b_v2_amendment2.py implements it, compiles, hard-gated behind the same go argument, NEVER RUN; original battery untouched; at most one battery may ever run. docs/lessons_learned.md: 10 evidence-forced lessons. Research website created (none existed): single-file dark terminal HTML with programme verdicts, T vs U3 strategy table, the 10 lessons, decision point, method; canvas 'AVFI-NQ Weekly Strategy Research' + durable copy pipeline/docs/site.html; deployable to Andrea's hosting, not publicly hosted by the programme. Programme now waits on Andrea: battery go/wait/veto, Amendment 2 sign-off (decides which battery), H3 annotations, TradingView cross-check, broker/CME export. Stage 6d (battery dry-run validation, 11 Nov 2026): found and fixed two crash bugs present in BOTH one-shot batteries since creation (eq.iloc[-1] and eq.cummax() on numpy arrays; mechanical fixes, no test changes; they would have crashed the real one-shot run). H11a VERDICT: the registered test is correctly sized (FP 5.8% at zero effect) but only detects implausible effects - power 7% at 0.10 sigma, 13% at 0.30, 22% at 0.50, 63% at 1.00, 96% at 1.50 weekly-sigma 4-week gaps; MDE at 80% power ~1.25 sigma (~4% absolute); the amendment's own honest prior is confirmed and quantified; a null result would be uninformative. H10a VERDICT: the AS-DRAFTED test (within-sample paired stationary block bootstrap, B2000/block13/demeaned/one-sided 5%) is INVALID on the registered series structure: measured FP 7-16% vs nominal 5%, because sp_z/sp_z_clv are near-integrated (lag-1 0.985, effective n ~2 over the 299-week window, predictors correlate 0.934) and the true sampling variance of the Fisher-z difference is ~2.2x the within-realization bootstrap variance; longer blocks do NOT fix it (FP 11-20% at blocks 13/67/130/260). Recommendation: do not sign Amendment 4 as drafted; the validated revision path is a persistence-preserving circular-shift (Theiler-style) alignment null (FP 5.3-6.7%, power 60-68% at true rho-difference 0.20, 97-100% at 0.30). Programme-level lesson: any correlation-style test on the smooth weekly registered series at n~300 inherits the same invalidity; future hypotheses must use persistence-preserving nulls or event-study machinery. Integrity: the readout script's first run had a surrogate bug (missing AR(1) innovation scale on the second predictor, inflating H10a FP to 27-68%); caught by the target-vs-realized validity checks, fixed before anything was shown or synced; all published numbers are corrected runs; H11a unaffected. - The inevitability question answered (12 Nov 2026, main conversation; session 27, PROGRESS block 35): Andrea asked whether, with a statistical true edge out of reach, a strategy could instead be built from "inevitable occurrences" or conditions "impossible to result in drawdown" (impulse-correction, crash-as-best-long, the last orderbook low before a never-remet break, a 5-minute time stop, compression inevitably preceding expansion), restating his standing belief that AVFI confers tops/bottoms information. Verdict, from the record: inevitability without timing and direction is not edge (true and priced); inevitability with both does not exist in real time - those points are hindsight identities, and any real-time condition for them collapses back into statistics; there is no third door. Item by item: impulse/correction and compression are true and ALREADY implemented as state-dependent sizing (V throttle, V4h target; lesson 10); crash-as-best-long is H7's lesson (one episode, null at the battery, Nikkei survivorship caveat); the never-remet low is breakout logic, i.e. rule set T, the only replicated effect, which failed the holdout; the time stop bounds time exposure, not loss, and is unoperable at 5-minute granularity in this programme (no intraday/orderbook data, once/day automation, costs dominate); "impossible to result in drawdown" has only the no-position instance (Patterson's funds are the case file for believing otherwise). The AVFI tops/bottoms belief was tested exactly as Andrea directed, pre-registered and signed (Amendment 3): H8 flow-fade divTop n=32 with 4-week forward returns ABOVE the control (p=0.79, family 0.89); H9 climax absorption n=3 events in 26 years (p=0.08/0.089, anecdote quota); bottoms null likewise; frozen nulls, never re-run. The belief is recorded as a belief; AVFI's residual role is a regime descriptor. Nothing registered from the inevitability framework; family cap and FTEP posture (few registrations, long tracks) stand.

The core algebra derived and verified on synthetic bars only (no market data): with the prior close inside the bar, the per-bar "delta" is exactly volume * ((high+low) - 2*prevClose) / (high-low) (max error 6.4e-9 over 200k bars) - the CLOSE never enters the formula, so the proxy is volume-weighted candle anatomy, not order flow; reversal bars are misread by construction (an upthrust closing on its lows prints as buying, the exact failure mode that matters for turning-point work), and gap bars are always signed in the gap direction (gap-up: +volume*(high-low)/(high-prevClose)), so overnight gaps mechanically pump the cumulative.
